>          An old member of TDT informed us, that they could have been made by
>          Eric (T.E.M), who lived in Apeldoorn/NL.
>          So far only one of these tunes has been found used in a demo and
>          it was credited to "RvLoon Software", hence moved out to
>          /DEMOS/M-R/Rider_in_the_Sky.sid. It is unknown if that credit is real,
>          or if also all these tunes were made by the same author.
>          If you have any info regarding this, please email the HVSC crew.
>
> TITLE: The Final Countdown [from The Final Countdown]
>  ARTIST: Europe
